\"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\",\n    \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\",\n]\n\nPROJECT_DIR = \"pythia_nucleus\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/features_all.jsonl\"),\n    \"gpt2xl\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/gpt2xl/features.jsonl\"),\n    \"olmo\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/olmo/features.jsonl\"),\n}\n\nTRUNC_LEN = 507\n\n\n# ── Feature computation (identical to training pipeline) ──\n\ndef compute_repetition(token_ids, n):\n    if len(token_ids) < n:\n        return 0.0\n    ngrams = [tuple(token_ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(token_ids) - n + 1)]\n    total = len(ngrams)\n    unique = len(set(ngrams))\n    return 1.0 - unique / total if total > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef compute_lexical_features(token_ids):\n    n_tokens = len(token_ids)\n    if n_tokens == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\",\n            \"trigram_entropy\", \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\"]}\n    counts = Counter(token_ids)\n    ttr = len(counts) / n_tokens\n    hapax = sum(1 for v in counts.values() if v == 1) / len(counts) if counts else 0.0\n\n    def ngram_entropy_val(ids, n):\n        if len(ids) < n:\n            return 0.0\n        ng = [tuple(ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(ids) - n + 1)]\n        c = Counter(ng)\n        total = sum(c.values())\n        probs = np.array([v / total for v in c.values()])\n        return float(entropy(probs))\n\n    return {\n        \"type_token_ratio\": ttr,\n        \"hapax_ratio\": hapax,\n        \"bigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 2),\n        \"trigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_2gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 2),\n        \"rep_3gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_4gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 4),\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_perplexity_features(losses_np):\n    if len(losses_np) == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n            \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n            \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\"]}\n\n    ppl = np.exp(losses_np)\n    result = {\n        \"mean_ppl\": float(np.mean(ppl)),\n        \"var_ppl\": float(np.var(ppl)),\n        \"skewness_ppl\": float(skew(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 2 else 0.0,\n        \"kurtosis_ppl\": float(kurtosis(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 3 else 0.0,\n        \"p10_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 10)),\n        \"p25_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 25)),\n        \"p50_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 50)),\n        \"p75_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 75)),\n        \"p90_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 90)),\n    }\n    surprisal = losses_np\n    result[\"mean_surprisal\"] = float(np.mean(surprisal))\n    result[\"var_surprisal\"] = float(np.var(surprisal))\n\n    if len(surprisal) > 1 and surprisal.max() > surprisal.min():\n        n_bins = min(50, max(10, len(surprisal) // 5))\n        hist, _ = np.histogram(surprisal, bins=n_bins, density=True)\n        bin_width = (surprisal.max() - surprisal.min()) / n_bins\n        probs = hist * bin_width\n        probs = probs[probs > 0]\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = float(entropy(probs))\n    else:\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = 0.0\n\n    return result\n\n\n# ── Data loading ──\n\ndef load_wild_texts(dataset_name, n_target, tokenizer, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load passages from HuggingFace streaming, filtering by min length.\"\"\"\n    from datasets import load_dataset\n\n    texts = []\n    skipped = 0\n\n    if dataset_name == \"fineweb\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"HuggingFaceFW/fineweb\", name=\"sample-10BT\",\n                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"text\"),\n            dict(path=\"HuggingFaceFW/fineweb\", name=\"default\",\n                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    elif dataset_name == \"refinedweb\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"tiiuae/falcon-refinedweb\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"content\"),\n            dict(path=\"allenai/c4\", name=\"en\", split=\"validation\",\n                 text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown dataset: {dataset_name}\")\n\n    ds = None\n    text_field = \"text\"\n\n    for cfg in configs:\n        try:\n            kwargs = dict(streaming=True)\n            if \"name\" in cfg:\n                kwargs[\"name\"] = cfg[\"name\"]\n            print(f\"    Trying {cfg['path']} (config={cfg.get('name', 'default')})...\", flush=True)\n            ds = load_dataset(cfg[\"path\"], split=cfg.get(\"split\", \"train\"), **kwargs)\n            text_field = cfg[\"text_field\"]\n            print(f\"    OK: loaded {cfg['path']}\", flush=True)\n            break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    Failed: {e}\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n    if ds is None:\n        print(f\"    ERROR: all configs failed for {dataset_name}\", flush=True)\n        return []\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for sample in ds:\n        if len(texts) >= n_target:\n            break\n        text = sample.get(text_field, \"\") or sample.get(\"text\", \"\") or sample.get(\"raw_content\", \"\")\n        if not text or not isinstance(text, str) or len(text) < 200:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        tok_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)\n        if len(tok_ids) < trunc_len:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        texts.append(text)\n        if len(texts) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            print(f\"    {dataset_name}: {len(texts)}/{n_target} \"\n                  f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {dataset_name}: collected {len(texts)} passages \"\n          f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n    return texts\n\n\n# ── Feature extraction ──\n\ndef extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load Pythia-1.4B on GPU and extract 19 features per passage.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Loading model on {device}...\", flush=True)\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, torch_dtype=torch.float16)\n    model.to(device)\n    model.eval()\n\n    features = []\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for i, text in enumerate(texts):\n        token_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)[:trunc_len]\n        input_ids = torch.tensor([token_ids], device=device)\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            logits = model(input_ids).logits\n\n        shift_logits = logits[:, :-1, :].contiguous()\n        shift_labels = input_ids[:, 1:].contiguous()\n        losses = torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(\n            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.size(-1)),\n            shift_labels.view(-1),\n            reduction=\"none\",\n        )\n        losses_np = losses.cpu().numpy()\n\n        feat = {}\n        feat.update(compute_perplexity_features(losses_np))\n        feat.update(compute_lexical_features(token_ids))\n        features.append(feat)\n\n        if (i + 1) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            rate = (i + 1) / elapsed\n            print(f\"    {device}: {i+1}/{len(texts)} ({rate:.1f} samples/s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {device}: done {len(features)} samples in {elapsed:.0f}s\", flush=True)\n\n    del model\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n    return features\n\n\n# ── RF training & classification ──\n\ndef load_features_jsonl(filepath):\n    entries = []\n    with open(filepath) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if line:\n                entries.append(json.loads(line))\n    return entries\n\n\ndef train_rf(entries, n_estimators=200, seed=42):\n    filtered = [e for e in entries if e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n    X = np.array([[e[f] for f in FEATURE_NAMES] for e in filtered])\n    y = np.array([e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered])\n    rf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=n_estimators, random_state=seed, n_jobs=-1)\n    rf.fit(X, y)\n    return rf\n\n\ndef classify_wild(rf, features_list):\n    X = np.array([[f[fname] for fname in FEATURE_NAMES] for f in features_list])\n    pred = rf.predict(X)\n    prob = rf.predict_proba(X)\n    n = len(pred)\n    pred_dist = Counter(int(p) for p in pred)\n    d0_rate = float((pred == 0).sum()) / n * 100\n    fpr = 100.0 - d0_rate\n    return {\n        \"n_samples\": n,\n        \"d0_accuracy\": round(d0_rate, 4),\n        \"fpr\": round(fpr, 4),\n        \"pred_distribution\": {f\"d{d}\": int(pred_dist.get(d, 0)) for d in range(4)},\n        \"pred_pct\": {f\"d{d}\": round(pred_dist.get(d, 0) / n * 100, 4) for d in range(4)},\n        \"mean_prob\": {f\"d{i}\": round(float(prob[:, i].mean()), 4) for i in range(min(4, prob.shape[1]))},\n        \"std_prob\": {f\"d{i}\": round(float(prob[:, i].std()), 4) for i in range(min(4, prob.shape[1]))},\n    }\n\n\n# ── Main ──\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--n_passages\", type=int, default=5000)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--trunc_len\", type=int, default=TRUNC_LEN)\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    t_start = time.time()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Plan 015 v2: Wild-Data Calibration\")\n    print(\"  RedPajama-v2 (cuda:0) & Dolma (cuda:1)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"  n_passages: {args.n_passages}\")\n    print(f\"  trunc_len:  {args.trunc_len}\")\n    print(f\"  model:      {MODEL_PATH}\")\n    print(f\"  output:     {OUTPUT_DIR}\")\n    n_gpus = torch.cuda.device_count()\n    for i in range(n_gpus):\n        print(f\"  GPU {i}: {torch.cuda.get_device_name(i)}\")\n    sys.stdout.flush()\n\n    # ── Step 1: Tokenizer ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 1: Loading Tokenizer\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH)\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\n    print(f\"  vocab_size={tokenizer.vocab_size}\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 2: Train 3 RF classifiers ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 2: Training 3 RF Classifiers\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    classifiers = {}\n    for name, fpath in FEATURE_FILES.items():\n        entries = load_features_jsonl(fpath)\n        filtered = [e for e in entries if e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n        depth_counts = Counter(e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered)\n        rf = train_rf(filtered, n_estimators=200, seed=args.seed)\n        classifiers[name] = rf\n errors = {}\n\n    def run_extraction(ds_name, gpu_id):\n        try:\n            device = f\"cuda:{gpu_id}\"\n            texts = wild_texts[ds_name]\n            if not texts:\n print(\"  Step 5: Classification Results\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    all_reports = {}\n    for ds_name, features in wild_features.items():\n        if not features:\n python3\n\"\"\"Plan 015 v2: Wild-Data Calibration on RedPajama-v2 & Dolma.\n\nValidates RF depth classifiers on large-scale wild web text.\n- 3 RF classifiers (Pythia nucleus, GPT-2 XL, OLMo)\n- 2 wild datasets: RedPajama-v2 (cuda:0), Dolma (cuda:1)\n- Feature extractor: Pythia-1.4B (consistent with training)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport numpy as np\nfrom collections import Counter\nfrom threading import Thread\nfrom scipy.stats import skew, kurtosis, entropy\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\nimport torch\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\n\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HOME\", \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET\", \"1\")\nfor k in (\"REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE\", \"SSL_CERT_FILE\"):\n    os.environ.setdefault(k, \"/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\")\n\nFEATURE_NAMES = [\n    \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n    \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n    \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\",\n    \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\",\n    \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\",\n]\n\nPROJECT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\"\nMODEL_PATH = \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\"\nOUTPUT_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"results/plan_015_wild_data_v2\")\n\nFEATURE_FILES = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/features_all.jsonl\"),\n    \"gpt2xl\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/gpt2xl/features.jsonl\"),\n    \"olmo\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/olmo/features.jsonl\"),\n}\n\nTRUNC_LEN = 507\n\n\n# ── Feature computation (identical to training pipeline) ──\n\ndef compute_repetition(token_ids, n):\n    if len(token_ids) < n:\n        return 0.0\n    ngrams = [tuple(token_ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(token_ids) - n + 1)]\n    total = len(ngrams)\n    unique = len(set(ngrams))\n    return 1.0 - unique / total if total > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef compute_lexical_features(token_ids):\n    n_tokens = len(token_ids)\n    if n_tokens == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\",\n            \"trigram_entropy\", \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\"]}\n    counts = Counter(token_ids)\n    ttr = len(counts) / n_tokens\n    hapax = sum(1 for v in counts.values() if v == 1) / len(counts) if counts else 0.0\n\n    def ngram_entropy_val(ids, n):\n        if len(ids) < n:\n            return 0.0\n        ng = [tuple(ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(ids) - n + 1)]\n        c = Counter(ng)\n        total = sum(c.values())\n        probs = np.array([v / total for v in c.values()])\n        return float(entropy(probs))\n\n    return {\n        \"type_token_ratio\": ttr,\n        \"hapax_ratio\": hapax,\n        \"bigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 2),\n        \"trigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_2gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 2),\n        \"rep_3gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_4gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 4),\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_perplexity_features(losses_np):\n    if len(losses_np) == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n            \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n            \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\"]}\n\n    ppl = np.exp(losses_np)\n    result = {\n        \"mean_ppl\": float(np.mean(ppl)),\n        \"var_ppl\": float(np.var(ppl)),\n        \"skewness_ppl\": float(skew(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 2 else 0.0,\n        \"kurtosis_ppl\": float(kurtosis(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 3 else 0.0,\n        \"p10_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 10)),\n        \"p25_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 25)),\n        \"p50_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 50)),\n        \"p75_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 75)),\n        \"p90_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 90)),\n    }\n    surprisal = losses_np\n    result[\"mean_surprisal\"] = float(np.mean(surprisal))\n    result[\"var_surprisal\"] = float(np.var(surprisal))\n\n    if len(surprisal) > 1 and surprisal.max() > surprisal.min():\n        n_bins = min(50, max(10, len(surprisal) // 5))\n        hist, _ = np.histogram(surprisal, bins=n_bins, density=True)\n        bin_width = (surprisal.max() - surprisal.min()) / n_bins\n        probs = hist * bin_width\n        probs = probs[probs > 0]\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = float(entropy(probs))\n    else:\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = 0.0\n\n    return result\n\n\n# ── Data loading ──\n\ndef load_wild_texts(dataset_name, n_target, tokenizer, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load passages from HuggingFace streaming, filtering by min length.\"\"\"\n    from datasets import load_dataset\n\n    texts = []\n    skipped = 0\n\n    if dataset_name == \"fineweb\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"HuggingFaceFW/fineweb\", name=\"sample-10BT\",\n                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"text\"),\n            dict(path=\"HuggingFaceFW/fineweb\", name=\"default\",\n                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    elif dataset_name == \"refinedweb\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"tiiuae/falcon-refinedweb\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"content\"),\n            dict(path=\"allenai/c4\", name=\"en\", split=\"validation\",\n                 text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown dataset: {dataset_name}\")\n\n    ds = None\n    text_field = \"text\"\n\n    for cfg in configs:\n        try:\n            kwargs = dict(streaming=True)\n            if \"name\" in cfg:\n                kwargs[\"name\"] = cfg[\"name\"]\n            print(f\"    Trying {cfg['path']} (config={cfg.get('name', 'default')})...\", flush=True)\n            ds = load_dataset(cfg[\"path\"], split=cfg.get(\"split\", \"train\"), **kwargs)\n            text_field = cfg[\"text_field\"]\n            print(f\"    OK: loaded {cfg['path']}\", flush=True)\n            break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    Failed: {e}\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n    if ds is None:\n        print(f\"    ERROR: all configs failed for {dataset_name}\", flush=True)\n        return []\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for sample in ds:\n        if len(texts) >= n_target:\n            break\n        text = sample.get(text_field, \"\") or sample.get(\"text\", \"\") or sample.get(\"raw_content\", \"\")\n        if not text or not isinstance(text, str) or len(text) < 200:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        tok_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)\n        if len(tok_ids) < trunc_len:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        texts.append(text)\n        if len(texts) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            print(f\"    {dataset_name}: {len(texts)}/{n_target} \"\n                  f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {dataset_name}: collected {len(texts)} passages \"\n          f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n    return texts\n\n\n# ── Feature extraction ──\n\ndef extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load Pythia-1.4B on GPU and extract 19 features per passage.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Loading model on {device}...\", flush=True)\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, torch_dtype=torch.float16)\n    model.to(device)\n    model.eval()\n\n    features = []\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for i, text in enumerate(texts):\n        token_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)[:trunc_len]\n        input_ids = torch.tensor([token_ids], device=device)\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            logits = model(input_ids).logits\n\n        shift_logits = logits[:, :-1, :].contiguous()\n        shift_labels = input_ids[:, 1:].contiguous()\n        losses = torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(\n            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.size(-1)),\n            shift_labels.view(-1),\n            reduction=\"none\",\n        )\n        losses_np = losses.cpu().numpy()\n\n        feat = {}\n        feat.update(compute_perplexity_features(losses_np))\n        feat.update(compute_lexical_features(token_ids))\n        features.append(feat)\n\n        if (i + 1) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            rate = (i + 1) / elapsed\n            print(f\"    {device}: {i+1}/{len(texts)} ({rate:.1f} samples/s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {device}: done {len(features)} samples in {elapsed:.0f}s\", flush=True)\n\n    del model\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n    return features\n\n\n# ── RF training & classification ──\n\ndef load_features_jsonl(filepath):\n    entries = []\n    with open(filepath) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if line:\n                entries.append(json.loads(line))\n    return entries\n\n\ndef train_rf(entries, n_estimators=200, seed=42):\n    filtered = [e for e in entries if e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n    X = np.array([[e[f] for f in FEATURE_NAMES] for e in filtered])\n    y = np.array([e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered])\n    rf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=n_estimators, random_state=seed, n_jobs=-1)\n    rf.fit(X, y)\n    return rf\n\n\ndef classify_wild(rf, features_list):\n    X = np.array([[f[fname] for fname in FEATURE_NAMES] for f in features_list])\n    pred = rf.predict(X)\n    prob = rf.predict_proba(X)\n    n = len(pred)\n    pred_dist = Counter(int(p) for p in pred)\n    d0_rate = float((pred == 0).sum()) / n * 100\n    fpr = 100.0 - d0_rate\n    return {\n        \"n_samples\": n,\n        \"d0_accuracy\": round(d0_rate, 4),\n        \"fpr\": round(fpr, 4),\n        \"pred_distribution\": {f\"d{d}\": int(pred_dist.get(d, 0)) for d in range(4)},\n        \"pred_pct\": {f\"d{d}\": round(pred_dist.get(d, 0) / n * 100, 4) for d in range(4)},\n        \"mean_prob\": {f\"d{i}\": round(float(prob[:, i].mean()), 4) for i in range(min(4, prob.shape[1]))},\n        \"std_prob\": {f\"d{i}\": round(float(prob[:, i].std()), 4) for i in range(min(4, prob.shape[1]))},\n    }\n\n\n# ── Main ──\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--n_passages\", type=int, default=5000)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--trunc_len\", type=int, default=TRUNC_LEN)\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    t_start = time.time()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Plan 015 v2: Wild-Data Calibration\")\n    print(\"  RedPajama-v2 (cuda:0) & Dolma (cuda:1)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"  n_passages: {args.n_passages}\")\n    print(f\"  trunc_len:  {args.trunc_len}\")\n    print(f\"  model:      {MODEL_PATH}\")\n    print(f\"  output:     {OUTPUT_DIR}\")\n    n_gpus = torch.cuda.device_count()\n    for i in range(n_gpus):\n        print(f\"  GPU {i}: {torch.cuda.get_device_name(i)}\")\n    sys.stdout.flush()\n\n    # ── Step 1: Tokenizer ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 1: Loading Tokenizer\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH)\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\n    print(f\"  vocab_size={tokenizer.vocab_size}\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 2: Train 3 RF classifiers ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 2: Training 3 RF Classifiers\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    classifiers = {}\n    for name, fpath in FEATURE_FILES.items():\n        entries = load_features_jsonl(fpath)\n        filtered = [e for e in entries if e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n        depth_counts = Counter(e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered)\n        rf = train_rf(filtered, n_estimators=200, seed=args.seed)\n        classifiers[name] = rf\n        print(f\"  {name}: {dict(sorted(depth_counts.items()))}, \"\n              f\"{rf.n_estimators} trees\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 3: Load wild data (sequential, network-bound) ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 3: Loading Wild Data (HuggingFace streaming)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    wild_texts = {}\n    for ds_name in [\"fineweb\", \"dolma\"]:\n        print(f\"\\n  --- {ds_name} ---\", flush=True)\n        wild_texts[ds_name] = load_wild_texts(\n            ds_name, args.n_passages, tokenizer, args.trunc_len)\n\n    # ── Step 4: Extract features (dual GPU parallel) ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 4: Feature Extraction (Dual GPU)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    wild_features = {}\n    errors = {}\n\n    def run_extraction(ds_name, gpu_id):\n        try:\n            device = f\"cuda:{gpu_id}\"\n            texts = wild_texts[ds_name]\n            if not texts:\n                wild_features[ds_name] = []\n                return\n            feats = extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, args.trunc_len)\n            wild_features[ds_name] = feats\n        except Exception as e:\n            import traceback\n            errors[ds_name] = traceback.format_exc()\n            wild_features[ds_name] = []\n\n    threads = []\n    gpu_map = {\"fineweb\": 0, \"dolma\": 1}\n    for ds_name, gpu_id in gpu_map.items():\n        t = Thread(target=run_extraction, args=(ds_name, gpu_id))\n        t.start()\n        threads.append(t)\n\n    for t in threads:\n        t.join()\n\n    for ds_name, err in errors.items():\n        print(f\"  ERROR in {ds_name}:\\n{err}\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 5: Classification ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 5: Classification Results\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    all_reports = {}\n    for ds_name, features in wild_features.items():\n        if not features:\n            print(f\"  Skipping {ds_name} (no features)\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n        print(f\"\\n  --- {ds_name.upper()} ({len(features)} passages) ---\", flush=True)\n        ds_reports = {}\n        for clf_name, rf in classifiers.items():\n            report = classify_wild(rf, features)\n            report[\"dataset\"] = ds_name\n            report[\"classifier\"] = clf_name\n            ds_reports[clf_name] = report\n            print(f\"    {clf_name:20s}  d0={report['d0_accuracy']:6.2f}%  \"\n                  f\"FPR={report['fpr']:6.2f}%  P(d0)={report['mean_prob']['d0']:.4f}\",\n                  flush=True)\n\n        all_reports[ds_name] = ds_reports\n\n    # ── Step 6: Cross-model consistency ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 6: Cross-Model FPR Consistency\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    consistency = {}\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        fprs = {c: r[\"fpr\"] for c, r in ds_reports.items()}\n        vals = list(fprs.values())\n        consistency[ds_name] = {\n            \"per_classifier\": fprs,\n            \"mean_fpr\": round(float(np.mean(vals)), 4),\n            \"std_fpr\": round(float(np.std(vals)), 4),\n            \"max_fpr\": round(float(np.max(vals)), 4),\n        }\n        print(f\"  {ds_name}: mean_FPR={consistency[ds_name]['mean_fpr']:.4f} \"\n              f\"+/- {consistency[ds_name]['std_fpr']:.4f}  \"\n              f\"(max={consistency[ds_name]['max_fpr']:.4f})\", flush=True)\n        for c, v in fprs.items():\n            print(f\"    {c}: {v:.4f}%\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 7: Save results ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 7: Saving Results\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    elapsed_total = time.time() - t_start\n\n    results = {\n        \"experiment\": \"wild_data_calibration_002\",\n        \"feature_model\": \"pythia-1.4b\",\n        \"rf_config\": {\"n_estimators\": 200, \"seed\": args.seed},\n        \"trunc_len\": args.trunc_len,\n        \"n_passages_requested\": args.n_passages,\n        \"elapsed_seconds\": round(elapsed_total, 1),\n        \"datasets\": {},\n        \"cross_model_consistency\": consistency,\n        \"classifier_cells\": list(FEATURE_FILES.keys()),\n    }\n\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        results[\"datasets\"][ds_name] = {\n            \"n_passages_loaded\": len(wild_texts.get(ds_name, [])),\n            \"n_features_extracted\": len(wild_features.get(ds_name, [])),\n            \"reports\": ds_reports,\n        }\n\n    results_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"calibration_results.json\")\n    with open(results_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"  Saved {results_path}\", flush=True)\n\n    for ds_name, features in wild_features.items():\n        if not features:\n            continue\n        feat_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR,                f.write(json.dumps(row) + \"\\n\")\n        print(f\"  Saved {feat_path} ({len(features)} rows)\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Final summary table ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  FINAL SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"\\n  {'Dataset':<15s} {'Classifier':<20s} {'N':>6s} \"\n          f\"{'d0%':>8s} {'FPR%':>8s} {'P(d0)':>8s}\")\n    print(f\"  {'-'*15}  RedPajama-v2 (cuda:0) & Dolma (cuda:1)","newString":"  FineWeb (cuda:0) & RefinedWeb (cuda:1)","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Plan 015 v2: Wild-Data Calibration on RedPajama-v2 & extractor: Pythia-1.4B (consistent with training)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport numpy as np\nfrom collections import Counter\nfrom threading import Thread\nfrom scipy.stats import skew, kurtosis, entropy\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\nimport torch\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\n\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HOME\", \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET\", \"1\")\nfor k in (\"REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE\", \"SSL_CERT_FILE\"):\n    os.environ.setdefault(k, \"/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\")\n\nFEATURE_NAMES = [\n    \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n    \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n    \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\",\n    \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\",\n    \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\",\n]\n\nPROJECT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\"\nMODEL_PATH = \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\"\nOUTPUT_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"results/plan_015_wild_data_v2\")\n\nFEATURE_FILES = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/features_all.jsonl\"),\n    \"gpt2xl\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/gpt2xl/features.jsonl\"),\n    \"olmo\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/olmo/features.jsonl\"),\n}\n\nTRUNC_LEN = 507\n\n\n# ── Feature computation (identical to training pipeline) ──\n\ndef compute_repetition(token_ids, n):\n    if len(token_ids) < n:\n        return 0.0\n    ngrams = [tuple(token_ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(token_ids) - n + 1)]\n    total = len(ngrams)\n    unique = len(set(ngrams))\n    return 1.0 - unique / total if total > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef compute_lexical_features(token_ids):\n    n_tokens = len(token_ids)\n    if n_tokens == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\",\n            \"trigram_entropy\", \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\"]}\n    counts = Counter(token_ids)\n    ttr = len(counts) / n_tokens\n    hapax = sum(1 for v in counts.values() if v == 1) / len(counts) if counts else 0.0\n\n    def ngram_entropy_val(ids, n):\n        if len(ids) < n:\n            return 0.0\n        ng = [tuple(ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(ids) - n + 1)]\n        c = Counter(ng)\n        total = sum(c.values())\n        probs = np.array([v / total for v in c.values()])\n        return float(entropy(probs))\n\n    return {\n        \"type_token_ratio\": ttr,\n        \"hapax_ratio\": hapax,\n        \"bigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 2),\n        \"trigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_2gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 2),\n        \"rep_3gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_4gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 4),\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_perplexity_features(losses_np):\n    if len(losses_np) == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n            \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n            \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\"]}\n\n    ppl = np.exp(losses_np)\n    result = {\n        \"mean_ppl\": float(np.mean(ppl)),\n        \"var_ppl\": float(np.var(ppl)),\n        \"skewness_ppl\": float(skew(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 2 else 0.0,\n        \"kurtosis_ppl\": float(kurtosis(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 3 else 0.0,\n        \"p10_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 10)),\n        \"p25_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 25)),\n        \"p50_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 50)),\n        \"p75_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 75)),\n        \"p90_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 90)),\n    }\n    surprisal = losses_np\n    result[\"mean_surprisal\"] = float(np.mean(surprisal))\n    result[\"var_surprisal\"] = float(np.var(surprisal))\n\n    if len(surprisal) > 1 and surprisal.max() > surprisal.min():\n        n_bins = min(50, max(10, len(surprisal) // 5))\n        hist, _ = np.histogram(surprisal, bins=n_bins, density=True)\n        bin_width = (surprisal.max() - surprisal.min()) / n_bins\n        probs = hist * bin_width\n        probs = probs[probs > 0]\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = float(entropy(probs))\n    else:\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = 0.0\n\n    return result\n\n\n# ── Data loading ──\n\ndef load_wild_texts(dataset_name, n_target, tokenizer, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load passages from HuggingFace streaming, filtering by min length.\"\"\"\n    from datasets import load_dataset\n\n    texts = []\n    skipped = 0\n\n    if dataset_name == \"fineweb\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"HuggingFaceFW/fineweb\", name=\"sample-10BT\",\n                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"text\"),\n            dict(path=\"HuggingFaceFW/fineweb\", name=\"default\",\n                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    elif dataset_name == \"refinedweb\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"tiiuae/falcon-refinedweb\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"content\"),\n            dict(path=\"allenai/c4\", name=\"en\", split=\"validation\",\n                 text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown dataset: {dataset_name}\")\n\n    ds = None\n    text_field = \"text\"\n\n    for cfg in configs:\n        try:\n            kwargs = dict(streaming=True)\n            if \"name\" in cfg:\n                kwargs[\"name\"] = cfg[\"name\"]\n            print(f\"    Trying {cfg['path']} (config={cfg.get('name', 'default')})...\", flush=True)\n            ds = load_dataset(cfg[\"path\"], split=cfg.get(\"split\", \"train\"), **kwargs)\n            text_field = cfg[\"text_field\"]\n            print(f\"    OK: loaded {cfg['path']}\", flush=True)\n            break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    Failed: {e}\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n    if ds is None:\n        print(f\"    ERROR: all configs failed for {dataset_name}\", flush=True)\n        return []\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for sample in ds:\n        if len(texts) >= n_target:\n            break\n        text = sample.get(text_field, \"\") or sample.get(\"text\", \"\") or sample.get(\"raw_content\", \"\")\n        if not text or not isinstance(text, str) or len(text) < 200:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        tok_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)\n        if len(tok_ids) < trunc_len:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        texts.append(text)\n        if len(texts) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            print(f\"    {dataset_name}: {len(texts)}/{n_target} \"\n                  f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {dataset_name}: collected {len(texts)} passages \"\n          f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n    return texts\n\n\n# ── Feature extraction ──\n\ndef extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load Pythia-1.4B on GPU and extract 19 features per passage.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Loading model on {device}...\", flush=True)\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, torch_dtype=torch.float16)\n    model.to(device)\n    model.eval()\n\n    features = []\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for i, text in enumerate(texts):\n        token_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)[:trunc_len]\n        input_ids = torch.tensor([token_ids], device=device)\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            logits = model(input_ids).logits\n\n        shift_logits = logits[:, :-1, :].contiguous()\n        shift_labels = input_ids[:, 1:].contiguous()\n        losses = torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(\n            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.size(-1)),\n            shift_labels.view(-1),\n            reduction=\"none\",\n        )\n        losses_np = losses.cpu().numpy()\n\n        feat = {}\n        feat.update(compute_perplexity_features(losses_np))\n        feat.update(compute_lexical_features(token_ids))\n        features.append(feat)\n\n        if (i + 1) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            rate = (i + 1) / elapsed\n            print(f\"    {device}: {i+1}/{len(texts)} ({rate:.1f} samples/s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {device}: done {len(features)} samples in {elapsed:.0f}s\", flush=True)\n\n    del model\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n    return features\n\n\n# ── RF training & classification ──\n\ndef load_features_jsonl(filepath):\n    entries = []\n    with open(filepath) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if line:\n                entries.append(json.loads(line))\n    return entries\n\n\ndef train_rf(entries, n_estimators=200, seed=42):\n    filtered = [e for e in entries if e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n    X = np.array([[e[f] for f in FEATURE_NAMES] for e in filtered])\n    y = np.array([e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered])\n    rf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=n_estimators, random_state=seed, n_jobs=-1)\n    rf.fit(X, y)\n    return rf\n\n\ndef classify_wild(rf, features_list):\n    X = np.array([[f[fname] for fname in FEATURE_NAMES] for f in features_list])\n    pred = rf.predict(X)\n    prob = rf.predict_proba(X)\n    n = len(pred)\n    pred_dist = Counter(int(p) for p in pred)\n    d0_rate = float((pred == 0).sum()) / n * 100\n    fpr = 100.0 - d0_rate\n    return {\n        \"n_samples\": n,\n        \"d0_accuracy\": round(d0_rate, 4),\n        \"fpr\": round(fpr, 4),\n        \"pred_distribution\": {f\"d{d}\": int(pred_dist.get(d, 0)) for d in range(4)},\n        \"pred_pct\": {f\"d{d}\": round(pred_dist.get(d, 0) / n * 100, 4) for d in range(4)},\n        \"mean_prob\": {f\"d{i}\": round(float(prob[:, i].mean()), 4) for i in range(min(4, prob.shape[1]))},\n        \"std_prob\": {f\"d{i}\": round(float(prob[:, i].std()), 4) for i in range(min(4, prob.shape[1]))},\n    }\n\n\n# ── Main ──\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--n_passages\", type=int, default=5000)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--trunc_len\", type=int, default=TRUNC_LEN)\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    t_start = time.time()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Plan 015 v2: Wild-Data Calibration\")\n    print(\"  RedPajama-v2 (cuda:0) & Dolma (cuda:1)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"  n_passages: {args.n_passages}\")\n    print(f\"  trunc_len:  {args.trunc_len}\")\n    print(f\"  model:      {MODEL_PATH}\")\n    print(f\"  output:     {OUTPUT_DIR}\")\n    n_gpus = torch.cuda.device_count()\n    for i in range(n_gpus):\n        print(f\"  GPU {i}: {torch.cuda.get_device_name(i)}\")\n    sys.stdout.flush()\n\n    # ── Step 1: Tokenizer ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 1: Loading Tokenizer\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH)\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\n    print(f\"  vocab_size={tokenizer.vocab_size}\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 2: Train 3 RF classifiers ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 2: Training 3 RF Classifiers\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    classifiers = {}\n    for name, fpath in FEATURE_FILES.items():\n        entries = load_features_jsonl(fpath)\n        filtered = [e for e in entries if e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n        depth_counts = Counter(e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered)\n        rf = train_rf(filtered, n_estimators=200, seed=args.seed)\n        classifiers[name] = rf\n        print(f\"  {name}: {dict(sorted(depth_counts.items()))}, \"\n              f\"{rf.n_estimators} trees\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 3: Load wild data (sequential, network-bound) ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 3: Loading Wild Data (HuggingFace streaming)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    wild_texts = {}\n    for ds_name in [\"fineweb\", \"refinedweb\"]:\n        print(f\"\\n  --- {ds_name} ---\", flush=True)\n        wild_texts[ds_name] = load_wild_texts(\n            ds_name, args.n_passages, tokenizer, args.trunc_len)\n\n    # ── Step 4: Extract features (dual GPU parallel) ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 4: Feature Extraction (Dual GPU)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    wild_features = {}\n    errors = {}\n\n    def run_extraction(ds_name, gpu_id):\n        try:\n            device = f\"cuda:{gpu_id}\"\n            texts = wild_texts[ds_name]\n            if not texts:\n                wild_features[ds_name] = []\n                return\n            feats = extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, args.trunc_len)\n            wild_features[ds_name] = feats\n        except Exception as e:\n            import traceback\n            errors[ds_name] = traceback.format_exc()\n            wild_features[ds_name] = []\n\n    threads = []\n    gpu_map = {\"fineweb\": 0, \"refinedweb\": 1}\n    for ds_name, gpu_id in gpu_map.items():\n        t = Thread(target=run_extraction, args=(ds_name, gpu_id))\n        t.start()\n        threads.append(t)\n\n    for t in threads:\n        t.join()\n\n    for ds_name, err in errors.items():\n        print(f\"  ERROR in {ds_name}:\\n{err}\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 5: Classification ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 5: Classification Results\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    all_reports = {}\n    for ds_name, features in wild_features.items():\n        if not features:\n            print(f\"  Skipping {ds_name} (no features)\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n        print(f\"\\n  --- {ds_name.upper()} ({len(features)} passages) ---\", flush=True)\n        ds_reports = {}\n        for clf_name, rf in classifiers.items():\n            report = classify_wild(rf, features)\n            report[\"dataset\"] = ds_name\n            report[\"classifier\"] = clf_name\n            ds_reports[clf_name] = report\n            print(f\"    {clf_name:20s}  d0={report['d0_accuracy']:6.2f}%  \"\n                  f\"FPR={report['fpr']:6.2f}%  P(d0)={report['mean_prob']['d0']:.4f}\",\n                  flush=True)\n\n        all_reports[ds_name] = ds_reports\n\n    # ── Step 6: Cross-model consistency ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 6: Cross-Model FPR Consistency\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    consistency = {}\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        fprs = {c: r[\"fpr\"] for c, r in ds_reports.items()}\n        vals = list(fprs.values())\n        consistency[ds_name] = {\n            \"per_classifier\": fprs,\n            \"mean_fpr\": round(float(np.mean(vals)), 4),\n            \"std_fpr\": round(float(np.std(vals)), 4),\n            \"max_fpr\": round(float(np.max(vals)), 4),\n        }\n        print(f\"  {ds_name}: mean_FPR={consistency[ds_name]['mean_fpr']:.4f} \"\n              f\"+/- {consistency[ds_name]['std_fpr']:.4f}  \"\n              f\"(max={consistency[ds_name]['max_fpr']:.4f})\", flush=True)\n        for c, v in fprs.items():\n            print(f\"    {c}: {v:.4f}%\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 7: Save results ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 7: Saving Results\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    elapsed_total = time.time() - t_start\n\n    results = {\n        \"experiment\": \"wild_data_calibration_002\",\n        \"feature_model\": \"pythia-1.4b\",\n        \"rf_config\": {\"n_estimators\": 200, \"seed\": args.seed},\n        \"trunc_len\": args.trunc_len,\n        \"n_passages_requested\": args.n_passages,\n        \"elapsed_seconds\": round(elapsed_total, 1),\n        \"datasets\": {},\n        \"cross_model_consistency\": consistency,\n        \"classifier_cells\": list(FEATURE_FILES.keys()),\n    }\n\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        results[\"datasets\"][ds_name] = {\n            \"n_passages_loaded\": len(wild_texts.get(ds_name, [])),\n            \"n_features_extracted\": len(wild_features.get(ds_name, [])),\n            \"reports\": ds_reports,\n        }\n\n    results_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"calibration_results.json\")\n    with open(results_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"  Saved {results_path}\", flush=True)\n\n    for ds_name, features in wild_features.items():\n        if not features:\n            continue\n        feat_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, f\"{ds_name}_features.jsonl\")\n        with open(feat_path, \"w\") as f:\n            for i, feat in enumerate(features):\n                row = {\"sample_id\": i}\n                row.update(feat)\n                f.write(json.dumps(row) + \"\\n\")\n        print(f\"  Saved {feat_path} ({len(features)} rows)\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Final summary table ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  FINAL SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"\\n  {'Dataset':<15s} {'Classifier':<20s} {'N':>6s} \"\n          f\"{'d0%':>8s} {'FPR%':>8s} {'P(d0)':>8s}\")\n    print(f\"  {'-'*15} python3\n\"\"\"Plan 015 v2: Wild-Data Calibration on RedPajama-v2 & Dolma.\n\nValidates RF depth classifiers on large-scale wild web text.\n- 3 RF classifiers (Pythia nucleus, GPT-2 XL, OLMo)\n- 2 wild datasets: RedPajama-v2 (cuda:0), Dolma (cuda:1)\n- Feature extractor: Pythia-1.4B (consistent with training)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport numpy as np\nfrom collections import Counter\nfrom threading import Thread\nfrom scipy.stats import skew, kurtosis, entropy\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\nimport torch\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\n\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HOME\", \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET\", \"1\")\nfor k in (\"REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE\", \"SSL_CERT_FILE\"):\n    os.environ.setdefault(k, \"/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\")\n\nFEATURE_NAMES = [\n    \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n    \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n    \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\",\n    \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\",\n    \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\",\n]\n\nPROJECT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\"\nMODEL_PATH = \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\"\nOUTPUT_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"results/plan_015_wild_data_v2\")\n\nFEATURE_FILES = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/features_all.jsonl\"),\n    \"gpt2xl\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/gpt2xl/features.jsonl\"),\n    \"olmo\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/olmo/features.jsonl\"),\n}\n\nTRUNC_LEN = 507\n\n\n# ── Feature computation (identical to training pipeline) ──\n\ndef compute_repetition(token_ids, n):\n    if len(token_ids) < n:\n        return 0.0\n    ngrams = [tuple(token_ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(token_ids) - n + 1)]\n    total = len(ngrams)\n    unique = len(set(ngrams))\n    return 1.0 - unique / total if total > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef compute_lexical_features(token_ids):\n    n_tokens = len(token_ids)\n    if n_tokens == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\",\n            \"trigram_entropy\", \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\"]}\n    counts = Counter(token_ids)\n    ttr = len(counts) / n_tokens\n    hapax = sum(1 for v in counts.values() if v == 1) / len(counts) if counts else 0.0\n\n    def ngram_entropy_val(ids, n):\n        if len(ids) < n:\n            return 0.0\n        ng = [tuple(ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(ids) - n + 1)]\n        c = Counter(ng)\n        total = sum(c.values())\n        probs = np.array([v / total for v in c.values()])\n        return float(entropy(probs))\n\n    return {\n        \"type_token_ratio\": ttr,\n        \"hapax_ratio\": hapax,\n        \"bigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 2),\n        \"trigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_2gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 2),\n        \"rep_3gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_4gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 4),\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_perplexity_features(losses_np):\n    if len(losses_np) == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n            \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n            \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\"]}\n\n    ppl = np.exp(losses_np)\n    result = {\n        \"mean_ppl\": float(np.mean(ppl)),\n        \"var_ppl\": float(np.var(ppl)),\n        \"skewness_ppl\": float(skew(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 2 else 0.0,\n        \"kurtosis_ppl\": float(kurtosis(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 3 else 0.0,\n        \"p10_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 10)),\n        \"p25_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 25)),\n        \"p50_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 50)),\n        \"p75_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 75)),\n        \"p90_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 90)),\n    }\n    surprisal = losses_np\n    result[\"mean_surprisal\"] = float(np.mean(surprisal))\n    result[\"var_surprisal\"] = float(np.var(surprisal))\n\n    if len(surprisal) > 1 and surprisal.max() > surprisal.min():\n        n_bins = min(50, max(10, len(surprisal) // 5))\n        hist, _ = np.histogram(surprisal, bins=n_bins, density=True)\n        bin_width = (surprisal.max() - surprisal.min()) / n_bins\n        probs = hist * bin_width\n        probs = probs[probs > 0]\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = float(entropy(probs))\n    else:\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = 0.0\n\n    return result\n\n\n# ── Data loading ──\n\ndef load_wild_texts(dataset_name, n_target, tokenizer, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load passages from HuggingFace streaming, filtering by min length.\"\"\"\n    from datasets import load_dataset\n\n    texts = []\n    skipped = 0\n\n    if dataset_name == \"fineweb\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"HuggingFaceFW/fineweb\", name=\"sample-10BT\",\n                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"text\"),\n            dict(path=\"HuggingFaceFW/fineweb\", name=\"default\",\n                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    elif dataset_name == \"refinedweb\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"tiiuae/falcon-refinedweb\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"content\"),\n            dict(path=\"allenai/c4\", name=\"en\", split=\"validation\",\n                 text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown dataset: {dataset_name}\")\n\n    ds = None\n    text_field = \"text\"\n\n    for cfg in configs:\n        try:\n            kwargs = dict(streaming=True)\n            if \"name\" in cfg:\n                kwargs[\"name\"] = cfg[\"name\"]\n            print(f\"    Trying {cfg['path']} (config={cfg.get('name', 'default')})...\", flush=True)\n            ds = load_dataset(cfg[\"path\"], split=cfg.get(\"split\", \"train\"), **kwargs)\n            text_field = cfg[\"text_field\"]\n            print(f\"    OK: loaded {cfg['path']}\", flush=True)\n            break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    Failed: {e}\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n    if ds is None:\n        print(f\"    ERROR: all configs failed for {dataset_name}\", flush=True)\n        return []\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for sample in ds:\n        if len(texts) >= n_target:\n            break\n        text = sample.get(text_field, \"\") or sample.get(\"text\", \"\") or sample.get(\"raw_content\", \"\")\n        if not text or not isinstance(text, str) or len(text) < 200:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        tok_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)\n        if len(tok_ids) < trunc_len:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        texts.append(text)\n        if len(texts) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            print(f\"    {dataset_name}: {len(texts)}/{n_target} \"\n                  f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {dataset_name}: collected {len(texts)} passages \"\n          f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n    return texts\n\n\n# ── Feature extraction ──\n\ndef extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load Pythia-1.4B on GPU and extract 19 features per passage.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Loading model on {device}...\", flush=True)\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, torch_dtype=torch.float16)\n    model.to(device)\n    model.eval()\n\n    features = []\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for i, text in enumerate(texts):\n        token_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)[:trunc_len]\n        input_ids = torch.tensor([token_ids], device=device)\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            logits = model(input_ids).logits\n\n        shift_logits = logits[:, :-1, :].contiguous()\n        shift_labels = input_ids[:, 1:].contiguous()\n        losses = torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(\n            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.size(-1)),\n            shift_labels.view(-1),\n            reduction=\"none\",\n        )\n        losses_np = losses.cpu().numpy()\n\n        feat = {}\n        feat.update(compute_perplexity_features(losses_np))\n        feat.update(compute_lexical_features(token_ids))\n        features.append(feat)\n\n        if (i + 1) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            rate = (i + 1) / elapsed\n            print(f\"    {device}: {i+1}/{len(texts)} ({rate:.1f} samples/s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {device}: done {len(features)} samples in {elapsed:.0f}s\", flush=True)\n\n    del model\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n    return features\n\n\n# ── RF training & classification ──\n\ndef load_features_jsonl(filepath):\n    entries = []\n    with open(filepath) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if line:\n                entries.append(json.loads(line))\n    return entries\n\n\ndef train_rf(entries, n_estimators=200, seed=42):\n    filtered = [e for e in entries if e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n    X = np.array([[e[f] for f in FEATURE_NAMES] for e in filtered])\n    y = np.array([e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered])\n    rf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=n_estimators, random_state=seed, n_jobs=-1)\n    rf.fit(X, y)\n    return rf\n\n\ndef classify_wild(rf, features_list):\n    X = np.array([[f[fname] for fname in FEATURE_NAMES] for f in features_list])\n    pred = rf.predict(X)\n    prob = rf.predict_proba(X)\n    n = len(pred)\n    pred_dist = Counter(int(p) for p in pred)\n    d0_rate = float((pred == 0).sum()) / n * 100\n    fpr = 100.0 - d0_rate\n    return {\n        \"n_samples\": n,\n        \"d0_accuracy\": round(d0_rate, 4),\n        \"fpr\": round(fpr, 4),\n        \"pred_distribution\": {f\"d{d}\": int(pred_dist.get(d, 0)) for d in range(4)},\n        \"pred_pct\": {f\"d{d}\": round(pred_dist.get(d, 0) / n * 100, 4) for d in range(4)},\n        \"mean_prob\": {f\"d{i}\": round(float(prob[:, i].mean()), 4) for i in range(min(4, prob.shape[1]))},\n        \"std_prob\": {f\"d{i}\": round(float(prob[:, i].std()), 4) for i in range(min(4, prob.shape[1]))},\n    }\n\n\n# ── Main ──\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--n_passages\", type=int, default=5000)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--trunc_len\", type=int, default=TRUNC_LEN)\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    t_start = time.time()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Plan 015 v2: Wild-Data Calibration\")\n    print(\"  FineWeb (cuda:0) & RefinedWeb (cuda:1)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"  n_passages: {args.n_passages}\")\n    print(f\"  trunc_len:  {args.trunc_len}\")\n    print(f\"  model:      {MODEL_PATH}\")\n    print(f\"  output:     {OUTPUT_DIR}\")\n    n_gpus = torch.cuda.device_count()\n    for i in range(n_gpus):\n        print(f\"  GPU {i}: {torch.cuda.get_device_name(i)}\")\n    sys.stdout.flush()\n\n    # ── Step 1: Tokenizer ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 1: Loading Tokenizer\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH)\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\n    print(f\"  vocab_size={tokenizer.vocab_size}\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 2: Train 3 RF classifiers ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 2: Training 3 RF Classifiers\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    classifiers = {}\n    for name, fpath in FEATURE_FILES.items():\n        entries = load_features_jsonl(fpath)\n        filtered = [e for e in entries if e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n        depth_counts = Counter(e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered)\n        rf = train_rf(filtered, n_estimators=200, seed=args.seed)\n        classifiers[name] = rf\n        print(f\"  {name}: {dict(sorted(depth_counts.items()))}, \"\n              f\"{rf.n_estimators} trees\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 3: Load wild data (sequential, network-bound) ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 3: Loading Wild Data (HuggingFace streaming)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    wild_texts = {}\n    for ds_name in [\"fineweb\", \"refinedweb\"]:\n        print(f\"\\n  --- {ds_name} ---\", flush=True)\n        wild_texts[ds_name] = load_wild_texts(\n            ds_name, args.n_passages, tokenizer, args.trunc_len)\n\n    # ── Step 4: Extract features (dual GPU parallel) ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 4: Feature Extraction (Dual GPU)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    wild_features = {}\n    errors = {}\n\n    def run_extraction(ds_name, gpu_id):\n        try:\n            device = f\"cuda:{gpu_id}\"\n            texts = wild_texts[ds_name]\n            if not texts:\n                wild_features[ds_name] = []\n                return\n            feats = extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, args.trunc_len)\n            wild_features[ds_name] = feats\n        except Exception as e:\n            import traceback\n            errors[ds_name] = traceback.format_exc()\n            wild_features[ds_name] = []\n\n    threads = []\n    gpu_map = {\"fineweb\": 0, \"refinedweb\": 1}\n    for ds_name, gpu_id in gpu_map.items():\n        t = Thread(target=run_extraction, args=(ds_name, gpu_id))\n        t.start()\n        threads.append(t)\n\n    for t in threads:\n        t.join()\n\n    for ds_name, err in errors.items():\n        print(f\"  ERROR in {ds_name}:\\n{err}\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 5: Classification ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 5: Classification Results\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    all_reports = {}\n    for ds_name, features in wild_features.items():\n        if not features:\n            print(f\"  Skipping {ds_name} (no features)\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n        print(f\"\\n  --- {ds_name.upper()} ({len(features)} passages) ---\", flush=True)\n        ds_reports = {}\n        for clf_name, rf in classifiers.items():\n            report = classify_wild(rf, features)\n            report[\"dataset\"] = ds_name\n            report[\"classifier\"] = clf_name\n            ds_reports[clf_name] = report\n            print(f\"    {clf_name:20s}  d0={report['d0_accuracy']:6.2f}%  \"\n                  f\"FPR={report['fpr']:6.2f}%  P(d0)={report['mean_prob']['d0']:.4f}\",\n                  flush=True)\n\n        all_reports[ds_name] = ds_reports\n\n    # ── Step 6: Cross-model consistency ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 6: Cross-Model FPR Consistency\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    consistency = {}\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        fprs = {c: r[\"fpr\"] for c, r in ds_reports.items()}\n        vals = list(fprs.values())\n        consistency[ds_name] = {\n            \"per_classifier\": fprs,\n            \"mean_fpr\": round(float(np.mean(vals)), 4),\n            \"std_fpr\": round(float(np.std(vals)), 4),\n            \"max_fpr\": round(float(np.max(vals)), 4),\n        }\n        print(f\"  {ds_name}: mean_FPR={consistency[ds_name]['mean_fpr']:.4f} \"\n              f\"+/- {consistency[ds_name]['std_fpr']:.4f}  \"\n              f\"(max={consistency[ds_name]['max_fpr']:.4f})\", flush=True)\n        for c, v in fprs.items():\n            print(f\"    {c}: {v:.4f}%\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 7: Save results ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 7: Saving Results\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    elapsed_total = time.time() - t_start\n\n    results = {\n        \"experiment\": \"wild_data_calibration_002\",\n        \"feature_model\": \"pythia-1.4b\",\n        \"rf_config\": {\"n_estimators\": 200, \"seed\": args.seed},\n        \"trunc_len\": args.trunc_len,\n        \"n_passages_requested\": args.n_passages,\n        \"elapsed_seconds\": round(elapsed_total, 1),\n        \"datasets\": {},\n        \"cross_model_consistency\": consistency,\n        \"classifier_cells\": list(FEATURE_FILES.keys()),\n    }\n\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        results[\"datasets\"][ds_name] = {\n            \"n_passages_loaded\": len(wild_texts.get(ds_name, [])),\n            \"n_features_extracted\": len(wild_features.get(ds_name, [])),\n            \"reports\": ds_reports,\n        }\n\n    results_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"calibration_results.json\")\n    with open(results_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"  Saved {results_path}\", flush=True)\n\n    for ds_name, features in wild_features.items():\n        if not features:\n            continue\n        feat_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, f\"{ds_name}_features.jsonl\")\n        with open(feat_path, \"w\") as f:\n            for i, feat in enumerate(features):\n                row = {\"sample_id\": i}\n                row.update(feat)\n                f.write(json.dumps(row) + \"\\n\")\n        print(f\"  Saved {feat_path} ({len(features)} rows)\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Final summary table ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  FINAL SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"\\n  {'Dataset':<15s} {'Classifier':<20s} {'N':>6s} \"\n          f\"{'d0%':>8s} {'FPR%':>8s} {'P(d0)':>8s}\")\n    print(f\"  {'-'*15} {'-'*20} {'-'*6} {'-'*8} {'-'*8} {'-'*8}\")\n\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        for clf_name, r in ds_reports.items():\n            print(f\"  {ds_name:<15s} {clf_name:<20s} {r['n_samples']:>6d} \"\n                  f\"{r['d0_accuracy']:>8.2f} {r['fpr']:>8.2f} \"\n                  f\"{r['mean_prob']['d0']:>8.4f}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n  Per-depth breakdown:\")\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        for clf_name, r in ds_reports.items():\n            pcts = r[\"pred_pct\"]\n            print(f\"    {ds_name}/{clf_name}: \"\n                  f\"d0={pcts['d0']:.2f}% d1={pcts['d1']:.2f}% \"\n                  f\"d2={pcts['d2']:.2f}% d3={pcts['d3']:.2f}%\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n  Total time: {elapsed_total:.0f}s\")\n    print(f\"  Results: {OUTPUT_DIR}/\")\n    print(\"  Done.\", flush=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n
